Install PostgreSQL 17 and add initdb, pg_ctl, and psql to PATH." >&2\n  exit 1\nfi\n\ndata_dir="$test_dir/data"\ncase "$(uname -s 2>/dev/null || true)" in\n  MINGW*|MSYS*|CYGWIN*)\n    if command -v cygpath >/dev/null 2>&1; then\n      data_dir="$(cygpath -w "$data_dir")"\n    fi\n    ;;\nesac\n\ncleanup() {\n  "$pg_ctl" -D "$data_dir" -m immediate stop >/dev/null 2>&1 || true\n  rm -rf "$test_dir"\n}\ntrap cleanup EXIT\n\n"$initdb" -D "$data_dir" -A trust -U postgres >/dev/null\n"$pg_ctl" -D "$data_dir" -o "-p $port -h 127.0.0.1" -w start >/dev/null\n\npsql=("$psql_bin" -h 127.0.0.1 -p "$port" -U postgres -d postgres -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1)\n"${psql[@]}" -f "$repo_dir/supabase/tests/booking/minimal_schema.sql" >/dev/null\nfor migration in \\\n  20260720160000_booking_phase2_atomic_admin.sql \\\n  20260720170000_booking_phase3_calendar_recovery.sql \\\n  20260720180000_booking_phase4_upload_idempotency.sql \\\n  20260720190000_booking_phase5_student_state.sql \\\n  20260720200000_booking_phase6_health.sql \\\n  20260810000000_booking_commerce_hub.sql \\\n  20260816010000_single_session_schema.sql \\\n  20260816020000_single_session_lifecycle.sql \\\n  20260830010000_booking_event_types.sql \\\n  20260830020000_slot_policy_reads_event_type.sql \\\n  20260914000000_scheduling_control_center.sql; do\n  "${psql[@]}" -f "$repo_dir/supabase/migrations/$migration" >/dev/null\ndone\n"${psql[@]}" -f "$repo_dir/supabase/tests/booking/single_session_offer_fixtures.sql" >/dev/null\n"${psql[@]}" -f "$repo_dir/supabase/migrations/20260816030000_single_session_workers_offers.sql" >/dev/null\n"${psql[@]}" -f "$repo_dir/supabase/tests/booking/single_session_lifecycle.sql" >/dev/null\n\n# Two different clients race the same native slot. The shared host-day advisory\n# lock plus the occupying exclusion constraint must let exactly one commit.\nrace_start="$("${psql[@]}" -Atc "SELECT race_start FROM public.single_session_test_runtime")"\nrace_sql_a="SELECT id FROM public.claim_single_session_slot(\'$race_start\'::timestamptz,\'Race A\',\'race-a@example.com\',NULL,\'[]\'::jsonb,\'idem-real-race-a\',repeat(\'1\',32),repeat(\'2\',32),NULL);"\nrace_sql_b="SELECT id FROM public.claim_single_session_slot(\'$race_start\'::timestamptz,\'Race B\',\'race-b@example.com\',NULL,\'[]\'::jsonb,\'idem-real-race-b\',repeat(\'3\',32),repeat(\'4\',32),NULL);"\nset +e\n"${psql[@]}" -c "$race_sql_a" >/dev/null 2>&1 &\nrace_pid_a=$!\n"${psql[@]}" -c "$race_sql_b" >/dev/null 2>&1 &\nrace_pid_b=$!\nwait "$race_pid_a"\nrace_status_a=$?\nwait "$race_pid_b"\nrace_status_b=$?\nset -e\nif { test "$race_status_a" -eq 0 && test "$race_status_b" -eq 0; } || \\\n   { test "$race_status_a" -ne 0 && test "$race_status_b" -ne 0; }; then\n  echo "native claim race did not produce exactly one winner" >&2\n  exit 1\nfi\nrace_winners="$("${psql[@]}" -Atc "SELECT count(*) FROM public.single_session_bookings WHERE client_idempotency_key IN (\'idem-real-race-a\',\'idem-real-race-b\')")"\ntest "$race_winners" = "1"\n\n# Cross-engine race: a package redemption and a native Hold contend for the\n# same clean slot. Both paths must use the same host-day advisory lock.\ncross_race_start="$("${psql[@]}" -Atc "SELECT cross_race_start FROM public.single_session_test_runtime")"\ncross_race_pass="$("${psql[@]}" -Atc "SELECT cross_race_pass_id FROM public.single_session_test_runtime")"\ncross_native_sql="SELECT id FROM public.claim_single_session_slot(\'$cross_race_start\'::timestamptz,\'Cross Native\',\'cross-native@example.com\',NULL,\'[]\'::jsonb,\'idem-real-cross-native\',repeat(\'5\',32),repeat(\'6\',32),NULL);"\ncross_package_sql="SELECT redemption_id FROM public.redeem_booking_pass_v2(\'$cross_race_pass\'::uuid,\'idem-real-cross-package\',\'$cross_race_start\'::timestamptz,NULL,\'Cross Package\',\'cross-package@example.com\');"\nset +e\n"${psql[@]}" -c "$cross_native_sql" >/dev/null 2>&1 &\ncross_pid_native=$!\n"${psql[@]}" -c "$cross_package_sql" >/dev/null 2>&1 &\ncross_pid_package=$!\nwait "$cross_pid_native"\ncross_status_native=$?\nwait "$cross_pid_package"\ncross_status_package=$?\nset -e\nif { test "$cross_status_native" -eq 0 && test "$cross_status_package" -eq 0; } || \\\n   { test "$cross_status_native" -ne 0 && test "$cross_status_package" -ne 0; }; then\n  echo "cross-engine claim race did not produce exactly one winner" >&2\n  exit 1\nfi\ncross_native_count="$("${psql[@]}" -Atc "SELECT count(*) FROM public.single_session_bookings WHERE client_idempotency_key=\'idem-real-cross-native\'")"\ncross_package_count="$("${psql[@]}" -Atc "SELECT count(*) FROM public.booking_pass_redemptions WHERE booking_pass_id=\'$cross_race_pass\' AND idempotency_key=\'idem-real-cross-package\'")"\ncross_used="$("${psql[@]}" -Atc "SELECT used_credits FROM public.booking_passes WHERE id=\'$cross_race_pass\'")"\ntest $((cross_native_count + cross_package_count)) -eq 1\ntest "$cross_used" = "$cross_package_count"\n\n"${psql[@]}" -f "$repo_dir/supabase/tests/booking/phase_concurrency.sql" >/dev/null\n\ncancel_sql="SELECT public.cancel_booking_redemption(\'20000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001\', true, NULL);"\n"${psql[@]}" -c "$cancel_sql" >/dev/null &\nfirst_pid=$!\n"${psql[@]}" -c "$cancel_sql" >/dev/null &\nsecond_pid=$!\nwait "$first_pid"\nwait "$second_pid"\n\nresult="$("${psql[@]}" -Atc "SELECT used_credits || \':\' || status FROM public.booking_passes WHERE id=\'10000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001\'")"\nrefund="$("${psql[@]}" -Atc "SELECT credit_refunded::text || \':\' || status || \':\' || calendar_action FROM public.booking_pass_redemptions WHERE id=\'20000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001\'")"\ntest "$result" = "0:active"\ntest "$refund" = "true:cancelled:delete"\n\necho "booking database migrations and double-cancel concurrency: PASS"\n\r\n'}]

## assistant

The review found real integrity gaps, and I’m fixing them before commit:

- A pass-creation undo could fail.
- An old undo could overwrite a newer change.
- Preview tokens were not bound to the operator and device.
- Preview/apply had a concurrency window.

One review suggestion was rejected because it conflicts with the approved safety rule: undoing a created link or pass will deactivate it, never permanently delete it. I’m making that behavior explicit and testing it.

## Tool: exec

const patch = "*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: C:\\Users\\Joshu\\dev\\ai-os\\projects\\lsatu\\docs\\superpowers\\plans\\2026-09-14-lsat-scheduling-control-center.md\n@@\n-- Produces `public.scheduling_preview(_action jsonb)`, `public.apply_scheduling_action(_action jsonb, _preview_token uuid, _actor_id uuid, _interface text, _device_name text)`, and `public.undo_scheduling_audit(_audit_id uuid, _actor_id uuid, _interface text, _device_name text)`.\n+- Produces `public.scheduling_preview(_action jsonb, _actor_id uuid, _interface text, _device_name text)`, `public.apply_scheduling_action(_action jsonb, _preview_token uuid, _actor_id uuid, _interface text, _device_name text)`, and `public.undo_scheduling_audit(_audit_id uuid, _actor_id uuid, _interface text, _device_name text)`.\n - `scheduling_preview` returns `{ token, expires_at, action, before, after, summary }` and stores a SHA-256 hash of the canonical action plus before snapshot. `apply_scheduling_action` rejects an expired or mismatched token.\n+- A preview is bound to the actor, interface, and device that created it. Preview/apply and undo serialize access to the same scheduling target and reject stale state instead of overwriting a newer change.\n - Undo never updates an existing audit row. It applies the inverse change and appends a new audit row whose `undo_of` points to the original entry; undone state is derived from that relationship.\n+- Undoing creation follows the global no-deletion rule: created events and passes are deactivated or revoked, not permanently removed.\n*** End Patch";
text(await tools.apply_patch(patch));

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 3.4 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': "CREATE TRIGGER scheduling_audit_no_update\r\n  BEFORE UPDATE ON public.scheduling_audit_log\r\n  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.reject_scheduling_audit_mutation();\r\nCREATE TRIGGER scheduling_audit_no_delete\r\n  BEFORE DELETE ON public.scheduling_audit_log\r\n  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION public.reject_scheduling_audit_mutation();\r\n\r\nCREATE TABLE public.scheduling_previews (\r\n  token uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\r\n  action jsonb NOT NULL,\r\n  before_state jsonb NOT NULL,\r\n  action_hash bytea NOT NULL,\r\n  actor_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id),\r\n  interface text NOT NULL CHECK (interface IN ('cli', 'mcp')),\r\n  device_name text NOT NULL,\r\n  expires_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now() + interval '5 minutes',\r\n  used_at timestamptz\r\n);\r\n\r\nCREATE FUNCTION public.scheduling_action_is_allowed(_kind text)\r\nRETURNS boolean LANGUAGE sql IMMUTABLE AS $$\r\n  SELECT _kind = ANY (ARRAY[\r\n    'event.create', 'event.update', 'event.deactivate',\r\n    'availability.replace', 'override.upsert', 'override.deactivate',\r\n    'pass.create', 'pass.update', 'pass.revoke',\r\n    'booking.cancel', 'booking.reschedule', 'booking.refund'\r\n  ]);\r\n$$;\r\n\r\nCREATE FUNCTION public.scheduling_target_lock_key(_action jsonb)\r\nRETURNS bigint LANGUAGE sql IMMUTABLE AS $$\r\n  SELECT ('x' || substr(md5((_action - 'values')::text), 1, 16))::bit(64)::bigint;\r\n$$;\r\n\r\nCREATE FUNCTION public.scheduling_action_before(_action jsonb)\r\nRETURNS jsonb LANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE SET search_path = public AS $$\r\nDECLARE\r\n  _kind text := _action->>'kind';\r\n  _schedule_id uuid;\r\n  _result jsonb;\r\nBEGIN\r\n  IF NOT public.scheduling_action_is_allowed(_kind) THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid_scheduling_action';\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  CASE split_part(_kind, '.', 1)\r\n    WHEN 'event' THEN\r\n      SELECT to_jsonb(t) INTO _result FROM public.booking_event_types t WHERE t.slug = _action->>'slug';\r\n    WHEN 'availability' THEN\r\n      _schedule_id := (_action->>'schedule_id')::uuid;\r\n      SELECT COALESCE(jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(a) - 'id' - 'created_at' ORDER BY a.day_of_week, a.start_time), '[]'::jsonb)\r\n      INTO _result FROM public.booking_availability a WHERE a.schedule_id = _schedule_id;\r\n    WHEN 'override' THEN\r\n      SELECT to_jsonb(o) INTO _result FROM public.booking_date_overrides o\r\n      WHERE o.schedule_id = (_action->>'schedule_id')::uuid AND o.local_date = (_action->>'local_date')::date;\r\n    WHEN 'pass' THEN\r\n      SELECT to_jsonb(p) - 'token_hash' INTO _result\r\n      FROM public.booking_passes p WHERE p.id = (_action->>'id')::uuid;\r\n    WHEN 'booking' THEN\r\n      SELECT to_jsonb(r) INTO _result\r\n      FROM public.booking_pass_redemptions r WHERE r.id = (_action->>'id')::uuid;\r\n  END CASE;\r\n  RETURN COALESCE(_result, 'null'::jsonb);\r\nEND;\r\n$$;\r\n\r\nCREATE FUNCTION public.scheduling_preview(\r\n  _action jsonb, _actor_id uuid, _interface text, _device_name text\r\n)\r\nRETURNS jsonb LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public AS $$\r\nDECLARE\r\n  _before jsonb;\r\n  _preview public.scheduling_previews%ROWTYPE;\r\n  _after jsonb;\r\nBEGIN\r\n  IF _interface NOT IN ('cli', 'mcp') OR NULLIF(trim(_device_name), '') IS NULL THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid_scheduling_actor';\r\n  END IF;\r\n  PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(public.scheduling_target_lock_key(_action));\r\n  _before := public.scheduling_action_before(_action);\r\n  _after := COALESCE(_action->'values', _action - 'kind');\r\n  INSERT INTO public.scheduling_previews (\r\n    action, before_state, action_hash, actor_id, interface, device_name\r\n  ) VALUES (\r\n    _action, _before,\r\n    digest(jsonb_build_array(_action, _before, _actor_id, _interface, _device_name)::text, 'sha256'),\r\n    _actor_id, _interface, _device_name\r\n  )\r\n  RETURNING * INTO _preview;\r\n  RETURN jsonb_build_object(\r\n    'token', _preview.token, 'expires_at', _preview.expires_at, 'action', _action,\r\n    'before', _before, 'after', _after,\r\n    'summary', format('%s %s', _action->>'kind', COALESCE(_action->>'slug', _action->>'id', _action->>'local_date', 'schedule'))\r\n  );\r\nEND;\r\n$$;\r\n\r\nCREATE FUNCTION public.apply_scheduling_action(\r\n  _action jsonb, _preview_token uuid, _actor_id uuid, _interface text, _device_name text\r\n) RETURNS jsonb LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public AS $$\r\nDECLARE\r\n  _preview public.scheduling_previews%ROWTYPE;\r\n  _kind text := _action->>'kind';\r\n  _values jsonb := COALESCE(_action->'values', '{}'::jsonb);\r\n  _before jsonb;\r\n  _after jsonb;\r\n  _audit_id uuid;\r\n  _schedule_id uuid;\r\nBEGIN\r\n  IF NOT public.scheduling_action_is_allowed(_kind) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid_scheduling_action'; END IF;\r\n  SELECT * INTO _preview FROM public.scheduling_previews WHERE token = _preview_token FOR UPDATE;\r\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'preview_not_found'; END IF;\r\n  IF _preview.used_at IS NOT NULL THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'preview_already_used'; END IF;\r\n  IF _preview.expires_at <= now() THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'preview_expired'; END IF;\r\n  IF _preview.actor_id <> _actor_id OR _preview.interface <> _interface\r\n     OR _preview.device_name <> _device_name THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'preview_actor_mismatch';\r\n  END IF;\r\n  PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(public.scheduling_target_lock_key(_action));\r\n  IF _preview.action <> _action\r\n     OR _preview.action_hash <> digest(\r\n       jsonb_build_array(_action, _preview.before_state, _actor_id, _interface, _device_name)::text,\r\n       'sha256')\r\n     OR public.scheduling_action_before(_action) <> _preview.before_state THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'preview_mismatch';\r\n  END IF;\r\n  _before := _preview.before_state;\r\n\r\n  CASE _kind\r\n    WHEN 'event.create' THEN\r\n      INSERT INTO public.booking_event_types (\r\n        slug, label, length_minutes, slot_interval_minutes, minimum_notice_minutes,\r\n        buffer_before_minutes, buffer_after_minutes, window_business_days,\r\n        price_cents, currency, requires_payment, active, schedule_id\r\n      ) VALUES (\r\n        _action->>'slug', _values->>'label', (_values->>'length_minutes')::int,\r\n        (_values->>'slot_interval_minutes')::int, COALESCE((_values->>'minimum_notice_minutes')::int, 1440),\r\n        COALESCE((_values->>'buffer_before_minutes')::int, 0), COALESCE((_values->>'buffer_after_minutes')::int, 0),\r\n        COALESCE((_values->>'window_business_days')::int, 6), COALESCE((_values->>'price_cents')::int, 0),\r\n        COALESCE(_values->>'currency', 'usd'), COALESCE((_values->>'requires_payment')::boolean, true),\r\n        COALESCE((_values->>'active')::boolean, true), (_values->>'schedule_id')::uuid\r\n      );\r\n    WHEN 'event.update' THEN\r\n      UPDATE public.booking_event_types SET\r\n        label = COALESCE(_values->>'label', label),\r\n        length_minutes = COALESCE((_values->>'length_minutes')::int, length_minutes),\r\n        slot_interval_minutes = CASE WHEN _values ? 'slot_interval_minutes' THEN (_values->>'slot_interval_minutes')::int ELSE slot_interval_minutes END,\r\n        minimum_notice_minutes = COALESCE((_values->>'minimum_notice_minutes')::int, minimum_notice_minutes),\r\n        buffer_before_minutes = COALESCE((_values->>'buffer_before_minutes')::int, buffer_before_minutes),\r\n        buffer_after_minutes = COALESCE((_values->>'buffer_after_minutes')::int, buffer_after_minutes),\r\n        window_business_days = COALESCE((_values->>'window_business_days')::int, window_business_days),\r\n        price_cents = COALESCE((_values->>'price_cents')::int, price_cents),\r\n        currency = COALESCE(_values->>'currency', currency),\r\n        requires_payment = COALESCE((_values->>'requires_payment')::boolean, requires_payment),\r\n        active = COALESCE((_values->>'active')::boolean, active),\r\n        schedule_id = COALESCE((_values->>'schedule_id')::uuid, schedule_id), updated_at = now()\r\n      WHERE slug = _action->>'slug';\r\n    WHEN 'event.deactivate' THEN\r\n      UPDATE public.booking_event_types SET active = false, updated_at = now() WHERE slug = _action->>'slug';\r\n    WHEN 'availability.replace' THEN\r\n      _schedule_id := (_action->>'schedule_id')::uuid;\r\n      DELETE FROM public.booking_availability WHERE schedule_id = _schedule_id;\r\n      INSERT INTO public.booking_availability (schedule_id, day_of_week, start_time, end_time)\r\n      SELECT _schedule_id, (r->>'day_of_week')::int, (r->>'start_time')::time, (r->>'end_time')::time\r\n      FROM jsonb_array_elements(COALESCE(_values->'ranges', '[]'::jsonb)) r;\r\n    WHEN 'override.upsert' THEN\r\n      INSERT INTO public.booking_date_overrides (schedule_id, local_date, closed, ranges, active)\r\n      VALUES ((_action->>'schedule_id')::uuid, (_action->>'local_date')::date,\r\n        COALESCE((_values->>'closed')::boolean, false), COALESCE(_values->'ranges', '[]'::jsonb), true)\r\n      ON CONFLICT (schedule_id, local_date) DO UPDATE SET closed = excluded.closed,\r\n        ranges = excluded.ranges, active = true, updated_at = now();\r\n    WHEN 'override.deactivate' THEN\r\n      UPDATE public.booking_date_overrides SET active = false, updated_at = now()\r\n      WHERE schedule_id = (_action->>'schedule_id')::uuid AND local_date = (_action->>'local_date')::date;\r\n    WHEN 'pass.create' THEN\r\n      INSERT INTO public.booking_passes (\r\n        id, student_id, student_email, student_name, token_hash, booking_slug,\r\n        total_credits, session_length_minutes, status, expires_at, notes, created_by\r\n      ) VALUES (\r\n        (_action->>'id')::uuid, (_values->>'student_id')::uuid,\r\n        _values->>'student_email', _values->>'student_name',\r\n        _values->>'token_hash', _values->>'booking_slug', (_values->>'total_credits')::int,\r\n        COALESCE((_values->>'session_length_minutes')::int, 70), 'active',\r\n        (_values->>'expires_at')::timestamptz, _values->>'notes', _actor_id\r\n      );\r\n    WHEN 'pass.update' THEN\r\n      UPDATE public.booking_passes SET status = COALESCE(_values->>'status', status),\r\n        expires_at = CASE WHEN _values ? 'expires_at' THEN (_values->>'expires_at')::timestamptz ELSE expires_at END,\r\n        notes = CASE WHEN _values ? 'notes' THEN _values->>'notes' ELSE notes END, updated_at = now()\r\n      WHERE id = (_action->>'id')::uuid;\r\n    WHEN 'pass.revoke' THEN\r\n      UPDATE public.booking_passes SET status = 'revoked', updated_at = now() WHERE id = (_action->>'id')::uuid;\r\n    WHEN 'booking.cancel' THEN\r\n      PERFORM public.cancel_booking_redemption((_action->>'id')::uuid, false, _actor_id);\r\n    WHEN 'booking.refund' THEN\r\n      PERFORM public.cancel_booking_redemption((_action->>'id')::uuid, true, _actor_id);\r\n    WHEN 'booking.reschedule' THEN\r\n      UPDATE public.booking_pass_redemptions SET start_at = (_values->>'start_at')::timestamptz,\r\n        end_at = (_values->>'end_at')::timestamptz, calendar_action = 'update', calendar_status = 'pending',\r\n        needs_gcal_sync = true, gcal_state = 'pending', updated_at = now()\r\n      WHERE id = (_action->>'id')::uuid AND status = 'booked';\r\n    ELSE\r\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid_scheduling_action';\r\n  END CASE;\r\n\r\n  _after := public.scheduling_action_before(_action);\r\n  UPDATE public.scheduling_previews SET used_at = now() WHERE token = _preview_token;\r\n  INSERT INTO public.scheduling_audit_log (actor_id, interface, device_name, kind, target, before_state, after_state)\r\n  VALUES (_actor_id, _interface, _device_name, _kind, _action - 'values' - 'kind', _before, _after)\r\n  RETURNING id INTO _audit_id;\r\n  RETURN jsonb_build_object('audit_id', _audit_id, 'before', _before, 'after', _after);\r\nEND;\r\n$$;\r\n\r\nCREATE FUNCTION public.undo_scheduling_audit(\r\n  _audit_id uuid, _actor_id uuid, _interface text, _device_name text\r\n) RETURNS jsonb LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public AS $$\r\nDECLARE\r\n  _original public.scheduling_audit_log%ROWTYPE;\r\n  _new_id uuid;\r\n  _current jsonb;\r\n  _target_action jsonb;\r\nBEGIN\r\n  SELECT * INTO _original FROM public.scheduling_audit_log WHERE id = _audit_id;\r\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'audit_not_found'; END IF;\r\n  IF _original.undo_of IS NOT NULL OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.scheduling_audit_log WHERE undo_of = _audit_id) THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'audit_not_undoable';\r\n  END IF;\r\n  IF split_part(_original.kind, '.', 1) NOT IN ('event', 'availability', 'override', 'pass') THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'audit_not_undoable';\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  _target_action := jsonb_build_object('kind', _original.kind) || _original.target;\r\n  PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(public.scheduling_target_lock_key(_target_action));\r\n  _current := public.scheduling_action_before(_target_action);\r\n  IF _current IS DISTINCT FROM _original.after_state THEN\r\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'audit_state_changed';\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  IF _original.kind LIKE 'event.%' THEN\r\n    IF _original.before_state = 'null'::jsonb THEN\r\n      UPDATE public.booking_event_types SET active = false, updated_at = now() WHERE slug = _original.target->>'slug';\r\n    ELSE\r\n      UPDATE public.booking_event_types SET label = _original.before_state->>'label',\r\n        length_minutes = (_original.before_state->>'length_minutes')::int,\r\n        slot_interval_minutes = (_original.before_state->>'slot_interval_minutes')::int,\r\n        minimum_notice_minutes = (_original.before_state->>'minimum_notice_minutes')::int,\r\n        buffer_before_minutes = (_original.before_state->>'buffer_before_minutes')::int,\r\n        buffer_after_minutes = (_original.before_state->>'buffer_after_minutes')::int,\r\n        window_business_days = (_original.before_state->>'window_business_days')::int,\r\n        price_cents = (_original.before_state->>'price_cents')::int,\r\n        currency = _original.before_state->>'currency',\r\n        requires_payment = (_original.before_state->>'requires_payment')::boolean,\r\n        active = (_original.before_state->>'active')::boolean,\r\n        schedule_id = (_original.before_state->>'schedule_id')::uuid, updated_at = now()\r\n      WHERE slug = _original.target->>'slug';\r\n    END IF;\r\n  ELSIF _original.kind = 'availability.replace' THEN\r\n    DELETE FROM public.booking_availability WHERE schedule_id = (_original.target->>'schedule_id')::uuid;\r\n    INSERT INTO public.booking_availability (schedule_id, day_of_week, start_time, end_time)\r\n    SELECT (_original.target->>'schedule_id')::uuid, (r->>'day_of_week')::int,\r\n      (r->>'start_time')::time, (r->>'end_time')::time FROM jsonb_array_elements(_original.before_state) r;\r\n  ELSIF _original.kind LIKE 'override.%' THEN\r\n    IF _original.before_state = 'null'::jsonb THEN\r\n      DELETE FROM public.booking_date_overrides WHERE schedule_id = (_original.target->>'schedule_id')::uuid\r\n        AND local_date = (_original.target->>'local_date')::date;\r\n    ELSE\r\n      INSERT INTO public.booking_date_overrides (schedule_id, local_date, closed, ranges, active)\r\n      VALUES ((_original.before_state->>'schedule_id')::uuid, (_original.before_state->>'local_date')::date,\r\n        (_original.before_state->>'closed')::boolean, _original.before_state->'ranges',\r\n        (_original.before_state->>'active')::boolean)\r\n      ON CONFLICT (schedule_id, local_date) DO UPDATE SET closed = excluded.closed,\r\n        ranges = excluded.ranges, active = excluded.active, updated_at = now();\r\n    END IF;\r\n  ELSE\r\n    IF _original.before_state = 'null'::jsonb THEN\r\n      UPDATE public.booking_passes SET status = 'revoked', updated_at = now()\r\n      WHERE id = (_original.target->>'id')::uuid;\r\n    ELSE\r\n      UPDATE public.booking_passes SET status = _original.before_state->>'status',\r\n        expires_at = (_original.before_state->>'expires_at')::timestamptz,\r\n        notes = _original.before_state->>'notes', updated_at = now()\r\n      WHERE id = (_original.target->>'id')::uuid;\r\n    END IF;\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  _current := CASE split_part(_original.kind, '.', 1)\r\n    WHEN 'event' THEN (SELECT to_jsonb(t) FROM public.booking_event_types t WHERE t.slug = _original.target->>'slug')\r\n    WHEN 'availability' THEN (SELECT COALESCE(jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(a) - 'id' - 'created_at' ORDER BY a.day_of_week, a.start_time), '[]'::jsonb) FROM public.booking_availability a WHERE a.schedule_id = (_original.target->>'schedule_id')::uuid)\r\n    WHEN 'override' THEN (SELECT to_jsonb(o) FROM public.booking_date_overrides o WHERE o.schedule_id = (_original.target->>'schedule_id')::uuid AND o.local_date = (_original.target->>'local_date')::date)\r\n    WHEN 'pass' THEN (SELECT to_jsonb(p) - 'token_hash' FROM public.booking_passes p WHERE p.id = (_original.target->>'id')::uuid)\r\n  END;\r\n  INSERT INTO public.scheduling_audit_log\r\n    (actor_id, interface, device_name, kind, target, before_state, after_state, undo_of)\r\n  VALUES (_actor_id, _interface, _device_name, 'undo.' || _original.kind, _original.target,\r\n    _original.after_state, COALESCE(_current, 'null'::jsonb), _original.id)\r\n  RETURNING id INTO _new_id;\r\n  RETURN jsonb_build_object('audit_id', _new_id, 'undo_of', _original.id, 'after', _current);\r\nEND;\r\n$$;\r\n\r\n-- A date override replaces weekly hours. Conflict checks remain in the claim RPC.\r\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.is_single_session_slot_bookable(\r\n  _start_at timestamptz, _now timestamptz DEFAULT now(), _event_slug text DEFAULT 'single-session'\r\n) RETURNS boolean LANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE SET search_path = public AS $$\r\nDECLARE\r\n  _event public.booking_event_types%ROWTYPE;\r\n  _tz text;\r\n  _length int;\r\n  _pitch int;\r\n  _local_start timestamp;\r\n  _local_date date;\r\n  _local_time time;\r\n  _override public.booking_date_overrides%ROWTYPE;\r\nBEGIN\r\n  SELECT * INTO _event FROM public.booking_event_types WHERE slug = _event_slug AND active;\r\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN RETURN false; END IF;\r\n  SELECT timezone INTO _tz FROM public.booking_schedules WHERE id = _event.schedule_id;\r\n  _tz := COALESCE(_tz, 'America/Toronto');\r\n  _length := _event.length_minutes;\r\n"}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 3.5 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': "    IF _business_days < _target THEN _day := _day + 1; END IF;\r\n  END LOOP;\r\n  RETURN _day;\r\nEND;\r\n$$;\r\n\r\n-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------\r\n-- Is this start time sellable: far enough out, inside the window, inside the\r\n-- weekly hours, and landing on the event's own slot grid.\r\n-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------\r\nDROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.is_single_session_slot_bookable(timestamptz, timestamptz);\r\n\r\nCREATE FUNCTION public.is_single_session_slot_bookable(\r\n  _start_at timestamptz,\r\n  _now timestamptz DEFAULT now(),\r\n  _event_slug text DEFAULT 'single-session'\r\n) RETURNS boolean\r\nLANGUAGE plpgsql STABLE SET search_path = public AS $$\r\nDECLARE\r\n  _tz text := COALESCE(\r\n    (SELECT timezone FROM public.booking_availability_config WHERE id = 1),\r\n    'America/Toronto'\r\n  );\r\n  _event public.booking_event_types%ROWTYPE;\r\n  _length integer;\r\n  _pitch integer;\r\n  _local_start timestamp;\r\n  _local_date date;\r\n  _local_time time;\r\nBEGIN\r\n  SELECT * INTO _event FROM public.booking_event_types WHERE slug = _event_slug AND active;\r\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN RETURN false; END IF;\r\n\r\n  _length := _event.length_minutes;\r\n  -- No explicit interval means slots start every length_minutes, which is what\r\n  -- Cal.com does and what produces the session's 1:00 / 2:10 / 3:20 chain.\r\n  _pitch := COALESCE(_event.slot_interval_minutes, _event.length_minutes);\r\n\r\n  IF _start_at IS NULL\r\n     OR _start_at < _now + make_interval(mins => _event.minimum_notice_minutes) THEN\r\n    RETURN false;\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  _local_start := _start_at AT TIME ZONE _tz;\r\n  _local_date := _local_start::date;\r\n  _local_time := _local_start::time;\r\n\r\n  IF _local_date < (_now AT TIME ZONE _tz)::date\r\n     OR _local_date > public.single_session_booking_window_end(_now, _event_slug) THEN\r\n    RETURN false;\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  RETURN EXISTS (\r\n    SELECT 1\r\n    FROM public.booking_availability a\r\n    WHERE a.day_of_week = extract(dow FROM _local_date)::integer\r\n      AND _local_time >= a.start_time\r\n      AND _local_time + make_interval(mins => _length) <= a.end_time\r\n      AND mod(\r\n        floor(extract(epoch FROM (_local_time - a.start_time)) / 60)::integer,\r\n        _pitch\r\n      ) = 0\r\n  );\r\nEXCEPTION WHEN OTHERS THEN\r\n  -- Invalid timezone/configuration is not permission to sell a slot.\r\n  RETURN false;\r\nEND;\r\n$$;\r\n\r\n-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------\r\n-- The claim. End time now comes from the event's length instead of a literal\r\n-- 70 minutes, and the booking records which event it is.\r\n-- --------------------------------------------------------------------------\r\nDROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.claim_single_session_slot(\r\n  timestamptz, text, text, text, jsonb, text, text, text, uuid);\r\n\r\nCREATE FUNCTION public.claim_single_session_slot(\r\n  _start_at timestamptz,\r\n  _name text,\r\n  _email text,\r\n  _notes text,\r\n  _guests jsonb,\r\n  _client_idempotency_key text,\r\n  _booking_token_hash text,\r\n  _ip_hash text,\r\n  _user_id uuid DEFAULT NULL,\r\n  _event_slug text DEFAULT 'single-session'\r\n) RETURNS public.single_session_bookings\r\nLANGUAGE plpgsql\r\nSECURITY DEFINER\r\nSET search_path = public\r\nAS $$\r\nDECLARE\r\n  _booking public.single_session_bookings%ROWTYPE;\r\n  _length integer := COALESCE(\r\n    (SELECT length_minutes FROM public.booking_event_types WHERE slug = _event_slug), 70);\r\n  _end_at timestamptz := _start_at + make_interval(mins => _length);\r\n  _email_normalized text := lower(trim(_email));\r\n  _guests_normalized jsonb;\r\n  _expired record;\r\nBEGIN\r\n  SELECT * INTO _booking FROM public.single_session_bookings\r\n  WHERE client_idempotency_key = _client_idempotency_key;\r\n  IF FOUND THEN\r\n    PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(public.single_session_slot_lock_key(_booking.start_at));\r\n    SELECT * INTO _booking FROM public.single_session_bookings WHERE id = _booking.id FOR UPDATE;\r\n    RETURN _booking;\r\n  END IF;\r\n\r\n  IF _start_at IS NULL OR NOT public.is_single_session_slot_bookable(_start_at, now(), _event_slug) THEN\r\n"}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 3.4 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': "\r\nCREATE TABLE public.gcal_sync_state (\r\n  id int PRIMARY KEY, calendar_id text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'primary', sync_token text,\r\n  last_synced_at timestamptz, last_full_sync_at timestamptz\r\n);\r\nINSERT INTO public.gcal_sync_state(id) VALUES (1);\r\n\r\nCREATE TABLE public.booking_availability_config (\r\n  id int PRIMARY KEY, timezone text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'America/Toronto', updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()\r\n);\r\nINSERT INTO public.booking_availability_config(id) VALUES (1);\r\nCREATE TABLE public.booking_availability (\r\n  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(), day_of_week int NOT NULL,\r\n  start_time time NOT NULL, end_time time NOT NULL, created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()\r\n);\r\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.get_booking_availability() RETURNS jsonb LANGUAGE sql AS $$\r\n  SELECT jsonb_build_object('timezone', c.timezone, 'ranges', COALESCE((\r\n    SELECT jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('day_of_week', a.day_of_week, 'start_time', a.start_time, 'end_time', a.end_time))\r\n    FROM public.booking_availability a\r\n  ), '[]'::jsonb)) FROM public.booking_availability_config c WHERE id = 1\r\n$$;\r\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.is_slot_bookable(timestamptz, int)\r\nRETURNS boolean LANGUAGE sql STABLE AS $$ SELECT true $$;\r\n\r\nCREATE TABLE public.external_work_uploads (\r\n  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(), student_id uuid, uploaded_by uuid,\r\n  file_name text NOT NULL, file_type text, storage_path text NOT NULL,\r\n  source_platform text, upload_type text, processing_status text,\r\n  booking_pass_id uuid REFERENCES public.booking_passes(id),\r\n  redemption_id uuid REFERENCES public.booking_pass_redemptions(id),\r\n"}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 3.5 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': "$$;\r\n\r\nDO $$\r\nBEGIN\r\n  IF NOT public.is_single_session_slot_bookable(\r\n    '2026-08-18 13:00 America/Toronto'::timestamptz,\r\n    '2026-08-16 10:00 America/Toronto'::timestamptz\r\n  ) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'anchored Tuesday slot should be bookable'; END IF;\r\n\r\n  IF public.is_single_session_slot_bookable(\r\n    '2026-08-18 13:30 America/Toronto'::timestamptz,\r\n    '2026-08-16 10:00 America/Toronto'::timestamptz\r\n  ) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'off-pitch slot should not be bookable'; END IF;\r\n\r\n  IF public.is_single_session_slot_bookable(\r\n    '2026-08-18 13:00 America/Toronto'::timestamptz,\r\n    '2026-08-17 14:00 America/Toronto'::timestamptz\r\n  ) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'slot inside 24-hour notice should not be bookable'; END IF;\r\n\r\n  IF public.is_single_session_slot_bookable(\r\n    '2026-08-25 13:00 America/Toronto'::timestamptz,\r\n    '2026-08-16 10:00 America/Toronto'::timestamptz\r\n  ) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'slot beyond six-business-day boundary should not be bookable'; END IF;\r\n\r\n  IF NOT public.is_single_session_slot_bookable(\r\n    '2026-11-02 14:00 America/Toronto'::timestamptz,\r\n    '2026-10-30 10:00 America/Toronto'::timestamptz\r\n  ) THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'DST boundary changed a valid host-local anchor'; END IF;\r\nEND;\r\n$$;\r\n\r\nCREATE TABLE public.single_session_test_runtime AS\r\nWITH config AS (\r\n  SELECT timezone, (now() AT TIME ZONE timezone)::date AS local_today\r\n  FROM public.booking_availability_config WHERE id = 1\r\n), candidates AS (\r\n  SELECT ((d.day::date + a.start_time) AT TIME ZONE c.timezone) AS start_at,\r\n    row_number() OVER (ORDER BY d.day, a.start_time) AS n\r\n  FROM config c\r\n  CROSS JOIN LATERAL generate_series(\r\n    c.local_today::timestamp,\r\n    public.single_session_booking_window_end(now())::timestamp,\r\n    interval '1 day'\r\n  ) d(day)\r\n  JOIN public.booking_availability a\r\n    ON a.day_of_week = extract(dow FROM d.day)::integer\r\n  WHERE a.end_time - a.start_time >= interval '5 hours 50 minutes'\r\n    AND ((d.day::date + a.start_time) AT TIME ZONE c.timezone) >= now() + interval '24 hours'\r\n)\r\nSELECT start_at AS base_start,\r\n  (SELECT start_at FROM candidates WHERE n = 2) AS race_start,\r\n  (SELECT start_at FROM candidates WHERE n = 3) AS cross_race_start,\r\n  NULL::uuid AS cross_race_pass_id\r\nFROM candidates WHERE n = 1;\r\n\r\n"}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 9.2 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '-- What a bookable event IS, as data instead of as constants.\n--\n-- The single session\'s shape -- 70 minutes, 24 hours\' notice, no buffers, a six\n-- business day window, $45 -- is currently written into the edge function AND\n-- into three SQL functions that enforce the slot policy inside the booking\n-- transaction. That is why there can only ever be one kind of session: a\n-- consultation is 30 minutes with 30-minute buffers and a five day window, and\n-- there is nowhere to say so.\n--\n-- This migration only creates the vocabulary and seeds it with the values that\n-- are live today. Nothing reads it yet, so this changes no behavior. The SQL\n-- policy functions are switched over to it next, one at a time.\n--\n-- Every number here was read from Joshua\'s Cal.com account on 2026-08-30, which\n-- stays the reference for what these events are supposed to be.\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.booking_event_types (\n  slug text PRIMARY KEY,\n  label text NOT NULL,\n  length_minutes int NOT NULL CHECK (length_minutes > 0),\n  -- Null means "start a slot every length_minutes", which is what Cal.com does\n  -- when no explicit interval is set. The single session\'s back-to-back 70\n  -- minute chain (1:00, 2:10, 3:20) is this rule, not a quirk.\n  slot_interval_minutes int CHECK (slot_interval_minutes IS NULL OR slot_interval_minutes > 0),\n  minimum_notice_minutes int NOT NULL DEFAULT 1440 CHECK (minimum_notice_minutes >= 0),\n  buffer_before_minutes int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (buffer_before_minutes >= 0),\n  buffer_after_minutes int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (buffer_after_minutes >= 0),\n  -- Business days size the window; days inside it are offered whenever the\n  -- weekly hours cover them, Saturday included.\n  window_business_days int NOT NULL DEFAULT 6 CHECK (window_business_days > 0),\n  price_cents int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (price_cents >= 0),\n  currency text NOT NULL DEFAULT \'usd\',\n  requires_payment boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT true,\n  active boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT true,\n  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\n  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()\n);\n\nINSERT INTO public.booking_event_types (\n  slug, label, length_minutes, slot_interval_minutes, minimum_notice_minutes,\n  buffer_before_minutes, buffer_after_minutes, window_business_days,\n  price_cents, currency, requires_payment\n) VALUES\n  (\'single-session\', \'LSAT Session\', 70, NULL, 1440, 0,  0,  6, 4500, \'usd\', true),\n  (\'consultation\',   \'Consultation\', 30, NULL, 1440, 30, 30, 5,    0, \'usd\', false)\nON CONFLICT (slug) DO NOTHING;\n\n-- One student, one price. Joshua already runs this by hand on Cal.com -- there\n-- are twelve event types on that account and most of them exist only because a\n-- particular person pays a particular rate. Keyed by email because the booker\n-- is not necessarily signed in when they book.\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.booking_price_overrides (\n  email text NOT NULL,\n  event_slug text NOT NULL REFERENCES public.booking_event_types(slug) ON DELETE CASCADE,\n  price_cents int NOT NULL CHECK (price_cents >= 0),\n  note text,\n  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\n  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\n  PRIMARY KEY (email, event_slug)\n);\n\nALTER TABLE public.booking_event_types ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\nALTER TABLE public.booking_price_overrides ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\n\n-- Students never read these directly: the booking page gets everything it needs\n-- from the edge function, which runs as service_role.\nCREATE POLICY "Admins manage booking event types"\n  ON public.booking_event_types FOR ALL TO authenticated\n  USING (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'))\n  WITH CHECK (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'));\n\nCREATE POLICY "Admins manage booking price overrides"\n  ON public.booking_price_overrides FOR ALL TO authenticated\n  USING (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'))\n  WITH CHECK (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'));\n\nGRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON public.booking_event_types TO authenticated;\nGRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON public.booking_price_overrides TO authenticated;\nGRANT ALL ON public.booking_event_types TO service_role;\nGRANT ALL ON public.booking_price_overrides TO service_role;\n\n-- The price a given booker pays for a given event: their override, else the\n-- event\'s own price. One definition, so the checkout and the payment\n-- verification can never disagree about what was owed.\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.booking_price_cents(_event_slug text, _email text)\nRETURNS integer\nLANGUAGE sql STABLE SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public AS $$\n  SELECT COALESCE(\n    (SELECT o.price_cents FROM public.booking_price_overrides o\n      WHERE o.event_slug = _event_slug AND lower(o.email) = lower(COALESCE(_email, \'\'))),\n    (SELECT t.price_cents FROM public.booking_event_types t WHERE t.slug = _event_slug)\n  );\n$$;\n\nREVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public.booking_price_cents(text, text) FROM public, anon, authenticated;\nGRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.booking_price_cents(text, text) TO service_role;\n\r\n'}]
